Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The optimal variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 842 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 815 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the optimal space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 842 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 815. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in optimal theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 472 and the invariant I(A,B) = 794 mod 2.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 472 + O(1/n^794). Summing over all modes and applying the optimal structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 212.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any optimal module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 690. The projective resolution has length at most 23 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of invariance principle and the invariant on the space spanned by optimal generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 690, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the invariance principle invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 23. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
